import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

class VerificarApiPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final Widget onSuccess;

  const VerificarApiPage({super.key, required this.onSuccess});

  @override
  State<VerificarApiPage> createState() => _VerificarApiPageState();
}

class _VerificarApiPageState extends State<VerificarApiPage> {
  bool carregando = true;
  String? erro;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    testarApi();
  }

  Future<void> testarApi() async {
    setState(() {
      carregando = true;
      erro = null;
    });

    try {
      final resp = await http
          .get(Uri.parse('http://localhost:5003/ping'))
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 5));

      if (resp.statusCode == 200) {
        Navigator.pushReplacement(
          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => widget.onSuccess),
        );
      } else {
        throw Exception('API respondeu com erro');
      }
    } catch (_) {
      setState(() {
        erro = 'Não foi possível conectar ao servidor.\n'
            'Verifique se a API está em execução.';
      });
    } finally {
      setState(() => carregando = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: Center(
        child: carregando
            ? const CircularProgressIndicator()
            : Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  const Icon(Icons.cloud_off, size: 64, color: Colors.red),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text(
                    erro ?? '',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),
                  ElevatedButton(
                    onPressed: testarApi,
                    child: const Text('Tentar novamente'),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}













import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

class IncluirProdutoDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final int empresa;
  final int cotacaoId;

  const IncluirProdutoDialog({
    super.key,
    required this.empresa,
    required this.cotacaoId,
  });

  @override
  State<IncluirProdutoDialog> createState() => _IncluirProdutoDialogState();
}

class _IncluirProdutoDialogState extends State<IncluirProdutoDialog> {
  final TextEditingController buscaCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final TextEditingController qtdCtrl = TextEditingController();

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> sugestoes = [];
  Map<String, dynamic>? produtoSelecionado;

  Future<void> buscarProdutos(String termo) async {
    if (termo.length < 3) return;

    final resp = await http.get(
      Uri.parse(
        'http://localhost:5003/produtos/busca?q=$termo&empresa=${widget.empresa}',
      ),
    );

    if (resp.statusCode == 200) {
      setState(() {
        sugestoes = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(jsonDecode(resp.body));
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> salvar() async {
    if (produtoSelecionado == null || qtdCtrl.text.isEmpty) return;

    await http.post(
      Uri.parse('http://localhost:5003/cotacoes/itens/incluir'),
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({
        'empresa': widget.empresa,
        'cotacao': widget.cotacaoId,
        'produto': produtoSelecionado!['produto'],
        'descricao': produtoSelecionado!['descricao'],
        'unidade': produtoSelecionado!['unidade'],
        'quantidade': int.parse(qtdCtrl.text),
      }),
    );

    Navigator.pop(context);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Incluir Produto'),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 500,
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            TextField(
              controller: buscaCtrl,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Buscar produto'),
              onChanged: buscarProdutos,
            ),
            if (sugestoes.isNotEmpty)
              Container(
                height: 150,
                child: ListView(
                  children: sugestoes.map((p) {
                    return ListTile(
                      title: Text(p['descricao']),
                      onTap: () {
                        setState(() {
                          produtoSelecionado = p;
                          sugestoes.clear();
                          buscaCtrl.text = p['descricao'];
                        });
                      },
                    );
                  }).toList(),
                ),
              ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            TextField(
              controller: qtdCtrl,
              keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Quantidade'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
          child: const Text('Cancelar'),
        ),
        ElevatedButton(
          onPressed: salvar,
          child: const Text('Incluir'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

















import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import '../../services/api_service.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

class IncluirFornecedorDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final int empresa;
  final int cotacaoId;
  final VoidCallback onIncluido;

  const IncluirFornecedorDialog({
    super.key,
    required this.empresa,
    required this.cotacaoId,
    required this.onIncluido,
  });

  @override
  State<IncluirFornecedorDialog> createState() =>
      _IncluirFornecedorDialogState();
}

class _IncluirFornecedorDialogState extends State<IncluirFornecedorDialog> {
  final TextEditingController _controller = TextEditingController();
  final FocusNode _focus = FocusNode();
  final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();
  final List<GlobalKey> _itemKeys = [];
  final FocusNode _focusLista = FocusNode();
  final FocusNode _focusIncluir = FocusNode();

  int _indexSelecionado = 0;

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> fornecedores = [];
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> filtrados = [];

  Map<String, dynamic>? selecionado;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    carregarFornecedores();

    Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 100), () {
      if (mounted) _focus.requestFocus();
    });
  }

  void _ajustarScroll() {
    const itemHeight = 56.0; // altura padrão do ListTile
    final position = _indexSelecionado * itemHeight;

    if (_scrollController.hasClients) {
      _scrollController.animateTo(
        position,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 120),
        curve: Curves.easeInOut,
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> carregarFornecedores() async {
    final resp = await http.get(
      Uri.parse(
        'http://localhost:5003/fornecedores?empresa=${widget.empresa}',
      ),
    );

    ApiService.validarResposta(resp);

    setState(() {
      fornecedores = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(jsonDecode(resp.body));
      filtrados = [];
    });
  }

  void _selecionarFornecedor(Map<String, dynamic> f) {
    setState(() {
      selecionado = f;
      _controller.text = f['razao'];
      filtrados = [];
    });

    _focusIncluir.requestFocus();
  }

  void _garantirVisivel({required bool descendo}) {
    if (_indexSelecionado < 0 || _indexSelecionado >= _itemKeys.length) return;

    final context = _itemKeys[_indexSelecionado].currentContext;
    if (context != null) {
      Scrollable.ensureVisible(
        context,
        alignment: descendo ? 1.0 : 0.0,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 120),
        curve: Curves.easeInOut,
      );
    }
  }

  void filtrar(String texto) {
    if (texto.trim().length < 3) {
      setState(() {
        filtrados = [];
        _indexSelecionado = 0;
        _itemKeys.clear();
      });
      return;
    }

    final t = texto.toLowerCase(); // ✅ FALTAVA ISSO

    setState(() {
      filtrados = fornecedores.where((f) {
        return (f['razao'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase().contains(t);
      }).toList();

      _indexSelecionado = 0;

      _itemKeys
        ..clear()
        ..addAll(List.generate(filtrados.length, (_) => GlobalKey()));
    });
  }

  Future<void> incluir() async {
    if (selecionado == null) return;

    try {
      await ApiService.post(
        'http://localhost:5003/cotacoes/fornecedores/incluir',
        {
          'empresa': widget.empresa,
          'cotacao': widget.cotacaoId,
          'fornecedor': selecionado!['id'],
        },
      );

      widget.onIncluido();
      Navigator.pop(context);

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Fornecedor incluído com sucesso'),
        ),
      );
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(e.message),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Incluir Fornecedor'),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 520,
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            TextField(
              controller: _controller,
              focusNode: _focus,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Fornecedor',
                hintText: 'Digite ao menos 3 letras',
              ),
              onChanged: filtrar,
              onSubmitted: (_) {
                if (filtrados.isNotEmpty) {
                  _focusLista.requestFocus();
                }
              },
            ),
            if (filtrados.isNotEmpty)
              Focus(
                focusNode: _focusLista,
                onKeyEvent: (node, event) {
                  if (event is KeyDownEvent) {
                    // ⬇️ seta para baixo
                    if (event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowDown) {
                      setState(() {
                        _indexSelecionado =
                            (_indexSelecionado + 1) % filtrados.length;
                      });
                      _garantirVisivel(descendo: true);
                      return KeyEventResult.handled;
                    }

                    // ⬆️ seta para cima
                    if (event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowUp) {
                      setState(() {
                        _indexSelecionado =
                            (_indexSelecionado - 1 + filtrados.length) %
                                filtrados.length;
                      });
                      _garantirVisivel(descendo: false);
                      return KeyEventResult.handled;
                    }

                    // ⏎ ENTER seleciona
                    if (event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.enter) {
                      final f = filtrados[_indexSelecionado];
                      _selecionarFornecedor(f);
                      return KeyEventResult.handled;
                    }
                  }

                  return KeyEventResult.ignored;
                },
                child: SizedBox(
                  height: 220,
                  child: ListView.builder(
                    controller: _scrollController,
                    itemCount: filtrados.length,
                    itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                      final f = filtrados[i];
                      final selecionadoLinha = i == _indexSelecionado;

                      return Container(
                        key: _itemKeys[i],
                        color: selecionadoLinha
                            ? Colors.yellow.shade300
                            : Colors.white,
                        child: ListTile(
                          title: Text(f['razao']),
                          onTap: () => _selecionarFornecedor(f),
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
          child: const Text('Cancelar'),
        ),
        ElevatedButton(
          focusNode: _focusIncluir,
          onPressed: incluir,
          child: const Text('Incluir'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _LogoCard extends StatelessWidget {

      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.80),

        border: Border.all(color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.10)),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.06),
            blurRadius: 10,
            offset: const Offset(0, 6),
          )
        ],
      ),
      child: Center(
        child: Container(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 520, maxHeight: 220),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.65),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
          ),
          child: Image.asset(
            imagePath,
            fit: BoxFit.contain,
            errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) =>
                const Icon(Icons.image_not_supported_outlined, size: 50),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// ✅ CARD FINAL
/// - 100% clicável
/// - 1 ícone apenas
/// - título central (sem "Acessar")
class _ActionCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final IconData icon;
  final VoidCallback onPressed;

  const _ActionCard({
    required this.title,
    required this.icon,
    required this.onPressed,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
      onTap: onPressed,
      hoverColor: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.03),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.80),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
          border: Border.all(color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.10)),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.06),
              blurRadius: 10,
              offset: const Offset(0, 6),
            )
          ],
        ),
        child: Center(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Icon(icon, size: 54),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              Text(
                title,
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 20,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                  height: 1.1,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}















import 'cotacao_fornecedores_page.dart';
import 'cotacao_produtos_page.dart';
import 'cotacao_nova_desktop_page.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../services/cotacao_service.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'dart:convert';

class CotacoesListDesktopPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const CotacoesListDesktopPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<CotacoesListDesktopPage> createState() =>
      _CotacoesListDesktopPageState();
}

class _CotacoesListDesktopPageState extends State<CotacoesListDesktopPage> {
  bool _loading = true;
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _cotacoes = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _carregarCotacoes();
  }

  Future<void> _gerarLinksVendedores(int cotacaoId) async {
    try {
      final resp = await http.post(
        Uri.parse('http://localhost:5003/cotacoes/fornecedores/gerar_links'),
        headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
        body: jsonEncode({
          'empresa': 1,
          'cotacao': cotacaoId,
        }),
      );

      if (resp.statusCode != 200) {
        throw Exception('Erro ao gerar links');
      }

      final data = jsonDecode(resp.body);

      if (data['ok'] != true) {
        throw Exception(data['erro'] ?? 'Erro desconhecido');
      }

      // 🔗 Por enquanto só informamos sucesso
      // depois podemos abrir WhatsApp, copiar links, etc.
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Links para vendedores gerados com sucesso'),
        ),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('Erro ao gerar links: $e'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _carregarCotacoes() async {
    setState(() => _loading = true);

    try {
      final lista = await CotacaoService.listarCotacoes(
        empresa: 1,
      );

      setState(() {
        _cotacoes = lista;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      setState(() => _loading = false);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('Erro ao carregar cotações: $e')),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _novaCotacao() async {
    final result = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (_) => const CotacaoNovaDesktopModal(),
    );

    if (result == true) {
      _carregarCotacoes();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF2F2F2), // cinza clarinho

      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Cotações'),
        actions: [
          ElevatedButton.icon(
            onPressed: _novaCotacao,
            icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
            label: const Text('Nova cotação'),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 16),
        ],
      ),
      body: _loading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : _cotacoes.isEmpty
              ? const Center(child: Text('Nenhuma cotação encontrada'))
              : Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  child: SingleChildScrollView(
                    child: DataTable(
                      columns: const [
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Nº')),
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Abertura')),
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Fechamento')),
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Status')),
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Descrição')),
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Ações')),
                      ],
                      rows: List.generate(_cotacoes.length, (index) {
                        final c = _cotacoes[index];

                        final bool linhaPar = index.isEven;

                        return DataRow(
                          color: MaterialStateProperty.all(
                            linhaPar ? const Color(0xFFFFF3C4) : Colors.white,
                          ),
                          cells: [
                            // Nº da cotação (6 dígitos)
                            DataCell(
                              Text(
                                c['cot_id'].toString().padLeft(6, '0'),
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                              ),
                            ),

                            // Data abertura
                            DataCell(
                              Text(c['data_abertura'] ?? ''),
                            ),

                            // Data fechamento
                            DataCell(
                              Text(c['data_fechamento'] ?? ''),
                            ),

                            // Status
                            DataCell(
                              Text(c['status'] ?? ''),
                            ),

                            // Descrição (mais larga)
                            DataCell(
                              SizedBox(
                                width: 260,
                                child: Text(
                                  c['descricao'] ?? '',
                                  maxLines: 1,
                                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),

                            // Ações
                            DataCell(
                              Row(
                                children: [
                                  IconButton(
                                    tooltip: 'Produtos da Cotação',
                                    icon: const Icon(Icons.playlist_add),
                                    onPressed: () {
                                      Navigator.push(
                                        context,
                                        MaterialPageRoute(
                                          builder: (_) => CotacaoProdutosPage(
                                            empresa:
                                                1, // mesma empresa usada no serviço
                                            cotacaoId: c[
                                                'cot_id'], // id da cotação da linha
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      );
                                    },
                                  ),

                                  IconButton(
                                    tooltip: 'Fornecedores da Cotação',
                                    icon: const Icon(Icons.store),
                                    onPressed: () {
                                      Navigator.push(
                                        context,
                                        MaterialPageRoute(
                                          builder: (_) =>
                                              CotacaoFornecedoresPage(
                                            empresa: 1,
                                            cotacaoId: c['cot_id'],
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      );
                                    },
                                  ),

                                  IconButton(
                                    tooltip: 'Enviar link para vendedores',
                                    icon: const Icon(
                                      Icons.send,
                                      color: Colors.green,
                                    ),
                                    onPressed: () {
                                      _gerarLinksVendedores(c['cot_id']);
                                    },
                                  ),

                                  // 🔜 outros botões depois (editar, fechar, excluir…)
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        );
                      }),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
    );
  }
}










import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:cotacao_online/services/api_service.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

class CotacaoProdutosPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final int empresa;
  final int cotacaoId;

  const CotacaoProdutosPage({
    super.key,
    required this.empresa,
    required this.cotacaoId,
  });

  @override
  State<CotacaoProdutosPage> createState() => _CotacaoProdutosPageState();
}

/* ============================================================
   DIALOG – INCLUIR PRODUTO
============================================================ */
class IncluirProdutoDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  final int empresa;
  final int cotacaoId;
  final VoidCallback onItemIncluido;

  const IncluirProdutoDialog({
    super.key,
    required this.empresa,
    required this.cotacaoId,
    required this.onItemIncluido,
  });

  @override
  State<IncluirProdutoDialog> createState() => _IncluirProdutoDialogState();
}

class _IncluirProdutoDialogState extends State<IncluirProdutoDialog> {
  final buscaCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final qtdCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final unidadeCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final precoCtrl = TextEditingController();

  final FocusNode buscaFocus = FocusNode();
  final FocusNode sugestoesFocus = FocusNode();
  final FocusNode qtdFocus = FocusNode();
  final FocusNode incluirFocus = FocusNode();

  final ScrollController sugestoesScrollCtrl = ScrollController();

  int sugestaoSelecionada = 0;
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> sugestoes = [];
  Map<String, dynamic>? produtoSelecionado;

  void _scrollParaSelecionado() {
    if (!sugestoesScrollCtrl.hasClients) return;

    const double itemHeight = 48;
    final viewport = sugestoesScrollCtrl.position.viewportDimension;

    final itemTop = sugestaoSelecionada * itemHeight;
    final itemBottom = itemTop + itemHeight;

    final scrollTop = sugestoesScrollCtrl.offset;
    final scrollBottom = scrollTop + viewport;

    if (itemTop < scrollTop) {
      sugestoesScrollCtrl.animateTo(
        itemTop,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 120),
        curve: Curves.easeOut,
      );
    } else if (itemBottom > scrollBottom) {
      sugestoesScrollCtrl.animateTo(
        itemBottom - viewport,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 120),
        curve: Curves.easeOut,
      );
    }
  }

  void _selecionarProduto(Map<String, dynamic> p) {
    setState(() {
      produtoSelecionado = p;
      sugestoes.clear();
      buscaCtrl.text = p['descricao'];
      unidadeCtrl.text = p['unidade'];
      precoCtrl.text = p['preco'].toString();
    });

    Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 50), () {
      qtdFocus.requestFocus();
    });
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      buscaFocus.requestFocus();
    });
  }

  Future<void> buscarProdutos(String termo) async {
    if (termo.length < 3) {
      setState(() => sugestoes = []);
      return;
    }

    final resp = await http.get(
      Uri.parse(
        'http://localhost:5003/produtos/busca'
        '?descricao=$termo'
        '&empresa=${widget.empresa}',
      ),
    );

    if (resp.statusCode == 200) {
      setState(() {
        sugestoes = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(jsonDecode(resp.body));
        sugestaoSelecionada = 0;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<void> salvar() async {
    if (produtoSelecionado == null || qtdCtrl.text.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Informe o produto e a quantidade'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    final qtd = int.tryParse(qtdCtrl.text);
    if (qtd == null || qtd <= 0) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Quantidade inválida'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      await ApiService.post(
        'http://localhost:5003/cotacoes/itens/incluir',
        {
          'empresa': widget.empresa,
          'cotacao': widget.cotacaoId,
          'produto': produtoSelecionado!['id'],
          'descricao': produtoSelecionado!['descricao'],
          'unidade': produtoSelecionado!['unidade'],
          'quantidade': qtd,
        },
      );

      // ✅ SUCESSO REAL
      widget.onItemIncluido();
      Navigator.pop(context);

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Item incluído com sucesso'),
        ),
      );
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      // ❌ erro controlado da API
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(e.message),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        ),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      // ❌ erro inesperado
      debugPrint('ERRO INESPERADO incluir item: $e');

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Erro inesperado ao incluir item'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AlertDialog(
      title: const Text('Incluir Produto'),
      content: SizedBox(
        width: 520,
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            TextField(
              focusNode: buscaFocus,
              controller: buscaCtrl,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Buscar produto (mín. 3 letras)',
              ),
              onChanged: buscarProdutos,
              onSubmitted: (_) =>
                  FocusScope.of(context).requestFocus(sugestoesFocus),
            ),
            if (sugestoes.isNotEmpty)
              Focus(
                focusNode: sugestoesFocus,
                onKey: (node, event) {
                  if (event is RawKeyDownEvent) {
                    if (event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowDown) {
                      setState(() {
                        if (sugestaoSelecionada < sugestoes.length - 1) {
                          sugestaoSelecionada++;
                          _scrollParaSelecionado();
                        }
                      });
                      return KeyEventResult.handled;
                    }

                    if (event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowUp) {
                      setState(() {
                        if (sugestaoSelecionada > 0) {
                          sugestaoSelecionada--;
                          _scrollParaSelecionado();
                        }
                      });
                      return KeyEventResult.handled;
                    }

                    if (event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.enter) {
                      _selecionarProduto(sugestoes[sugestaoSelecionada]);
                      return KeyEventResult.handled;
                    }
                  }
                  return KeyEventResult.handled;
                },
                child: Container(
                  height: 180,
                  margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    border: Border.all(color: Colors.black12),
                  ),
                  child: ListView.builder(
                    controller: sugestoesScrollCtrl,
                    itemExtent: 48,
                    itemCount: sugestoes.length,
                    itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                      final p = sugestoes[index];
                      final selecionado = index == sugestaoSelecionada;

                      return Container(
                        color: selecionado
                            ? const Color(0xFFFFF3C4)
                            : Colors.transparent,
                        child: ListTile(
                          dense: true,
                          title: Text(
                            p['descricao'],
                            maxLines: 1,
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          ),
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: TextField(
                    controller: unidadeCtrl,
                    enabled: false,
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Unidade'),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: TextField(
                    controller: precoCtrl,
                    enabled: false,
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Preço'),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: TextField(
                    controller: qtdCtrl,
                    focusNode: qtdFocus,
                    keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Quantidade'),
                    onSubmitted: (_) => incluirFocus.requestFocus(),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      actions: [
        TextButton(
          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
          child: const Text('Fechar'),
        ),
        ElevatedButton(
          focusNode: incluirFocus,
          onPressed: salvar,
          child: const Text('Incluir'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   TELA PRINCIPAL – GRID
============================================================ */
class _CotacaoProdutosPageState extends State<CotacaoProdutosPage> {
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> itens = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    carregarItens();
  }

  Future<void> carregarItens() async {
    final resp = await http.get(
      Uri.parse(
        'http://localhost:5003/cotacoes/${widget.cotacaoId}/itens'
        '?empresa=${widget.empresa}',
      ),
    );

    ApiService.validarResposta(resp);

    setState(() {
      itens = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(jsonDecode(resp.body));
    });
  }

  String get contadorItens => itens.length.toString().padLeft(3, '0');

  Future<void> _editarQuantidade(Map<String, dynamic> item) async {
    final TextEditingController ctrl =
        TextEditingController(text: item['quantidade'].toString());

    final FocusNode quantidadeFocus = FocusNode();
    final FocusNode salvarFocus = FocusNode();

    await showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (ctx) {
        return AlertDialog(
          title: const Text('Editar Quantidade'),
          content: TextField(
            controller: ctrl,
            focusNode: quantidadeFocus,
            autofocus: true,
            keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
            textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,

            // ENTER → vai para o botão Salvar
            onSubmitted: (_) => salvarFocus.requestFocus(),

            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              labelText: 'Quantidade',
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
              child: const Text('Cancelar'),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              focusNode: salvarFocus,
              child: const Text('Salvar'),
              onPressed: () async {
                final novaQtd = int.tryParse(ctrl.text);
                if (novaQtd == null) {
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    const SnackBar(
                      content: Text('Quantidade inválida'),
                      backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                    ),
                  );
                  return;
                }

                try {
                  await ApiService.post(
                    'http://localhost:5003/cotacoes/itens/alterar_quantidade',
                    {
                      'empresa': widget.empresa,
                      'cotacao': widget.cotacaoId,
                      'sequencia': item['sequencia'],
                      'quantidade': novaQtd,
                    },
                  );

                  // ✅ SUCESSO REAL
                  await carregarItens();
                  Navigator.pop(ctx);

                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    const SnackBar(
                      content: Text('Quantidade alterada com sucesso'),
                    ),
                  );
                } on ApiException catch (e) {
                  // 🔴 ERRO CONTROLADO DA API
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(
                      content: Text(e.message),
                      backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                    ),
                  );
                } catch (e) {
                  // 🔴 ERRO INESPERADO
                  debugPrint('ERRO INESPERADO: $e');
                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                    const SnackBar(
                      content: Text('Erro inesperado ao alterar quantidade'),
                      backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                    ),
                  );
                }
              },
            ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> _excluirItem(Map<String, dynamic> item) async {
    await showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (ctx) {
        bool carregando = false;

        return StatefulBuilder(
          builder: (ctx, setState) {
            return AlertDialog(
              title: const Text('Confirmar exclusão'),
              content: carregando
                  ? const SizedBox(
                      height: 48,
                      child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                    )
                  : const Text('Deseja realmente excluir este item?'),
              actions: [
                TextButton(
                  onPressed: carregando ? null : () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
                  child: const Text('Cancelar'),
                ),
                ElevatedButton(
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                  ),
                  onPressed: carregando
                      ? null
                      : () async {
                          setState(() => carregando = true);

                          try {
                            await ApiService.post(
                              'http://localhost:5003/cotacoes/itens/excluir',
                              {
                                'empresa': widget.empresa,
                                'cotacao': widget.cotacaoId,
                                'sequencia': item['sequencia'],
                              },
                            );

                            await carregarItens();

                            Navigator.pop(ctx);

                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              const SnackBar(
                                content: Text('Item excluído com sucesso'),
                              ),
                            );
                          } on ApiException catch (e) {
                            setState(() => carregando = false);

                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              SnackBar(
                                content: Text(e.message),
                                backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                              ),
                            );
                          } catch (e) {
                            setState(() => carregando = false);

                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              const SnackBar(
                                content:
                                    Text('Erro inesperado ao excluir item'),
                                backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                              ),
                            );
                          }
                        },
                  child: const Text('Excluir'),
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> _abrirIncluirProduto() async {
    await showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (_) => IncluirProdutoDialog(
        empresa: widget.empresa,
        cotacaoId: widget.cotacaoId,
        onItemIncluido: carregarItens,
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return FocusableActionDetector(
      autofocus: true,
      shortcuts: const {
        SingleActivator(LogicalKeyboardKey.f2): ActivateIntent(),
      },
      actions: {
        ActivateIntent: CallbackAction<Intent>(
          onInvoke: (_) {
            _abrirIncluirProduto();
            return null;
          },
        ),
      },
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF2F2F2),
        body: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Center(
            child: ConstrainedBox(
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 1200),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      // 🔙 Voltar (lado esquerdo)
                      TextButton.icon(
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
                        label: const Text('Voltar'),
                      ),

                      // 🧠 Centro real
                      Expanded(
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                          children: [
                            const Text(
                              'Produtos',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                fontSize: 16,
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: 24),
                            Text(
                              'Qtde: $contadorItens',
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                fontSize: 15,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),

                      // ➕ Incluir (lado direito)
                      ElevatedButton.icon(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                        label: const Text('Incluir - F2'),
                        onPressed: _abrirIncluirProduto,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: DataTable(
                      columns: const [
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Código')),
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Produto')),
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Unidade')),
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Preço')),
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Qtd')),
                        DataColumn(label: Text('Ações')),
                      ],
                      rows: List.generate(itens.length, (index) {
                        final item = itens[index];
                        final linhaPar = index.isEven;

                        return DataRow(
                          color: MaterialStateProperty.all(
                            linhaPar ? const Color(0xFFFFF3C4) : Colors.white,
                          ),
                          cells: [
                            DataCell(Text(item['produto'].toString())),
                            DataCell(Text(item['descricao'] ?? '')),
                            DataCell(Text(item['unidade'] ?? '')),
                            DataCell(
                                Text((item['preco'] ?? 0).toStringAsFixed(2))),
                            DataCell(Text(item['quantidade'].toString())),
                            DataCell(
                              Row(
                                children: [
                                  IconButton(
                                    icon: const Icon(Icons.edit,
                                        color: Colors.blue),
                                    onPressed: () => _editarQuantidade(item),
                                  ),
                                  IconButton(
                                    icon: const Icon(Icons.delete,
                                        color: Colors.red),
                                    onPressed: () => _excluirItem(item),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        );
                      }),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}













import '../../services/cotacao_service.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:cotacao_online/app/app_config.dart';

class CotacaoNovaDesktopModal extends StatefulWidget {
  const CotacaoNovaDesktopModal({super.key});

  @override
  State<CotacaoNovaDesktopModal> createState() =>
      _CotacaoNovaDesktopModalState();
}

class _CotacaoNovaDesktopModalState extends State<CotacaoNovaDesktopModal> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final TextEditingController descricaoCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _dtAberturaCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _dtFechamentoCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final FocusNode _focusDescricao = FocusNode();

  final _focusDtAbertura = FocusNode();
  final _focusDtFechamento = FocusNode();
  final _focusSalvar = FocusNode();

  String? _numeroCotacao;
  bool _podeSalvar = false;

  bool _validarAbertura() {
    if (!_dataValida(_dtAberturaCtrl.text)) {
      _mostrarErro('Data de abertura inválida');
      _focusDtAbertura.requestFocus();
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  bool _validarFechamento() {
    if (!_dataValida(_dtFechamentoCtrl.text)) {
      _mostrarErro('Data de fechamento inválida');
      _focusDtFechamento.requestFocus();
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  bool _validarFormularioCompleto() {
    if (!_validarAbertura()) return false;
    if (!_validarFechamento()) return false;

    setState(() => _podeSalvar = true);
    return true;
  }

  bool _dataValida(String v) {
    if (v.length != 10) return false;
    final partes = v.split('/');
    if (partes.length != 3) return false;

    final d = int.tryParse(partes[0]);
    final m = int.tryParse(partes[1]);
    final a = int.tryParse(partes[2]);

    if (d == null || m == null || a == null) return false;

    try {
      DateTime(a, m, d);
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    descricaoCtrl.dispose();
    _focusDescricao.dispose(); // 👈 ADICIONAR

    _dtAberturaCtrl.dispose();
    _dtFechamentoCtrl.dispose();

    _focusDtAbertura.dispose();
    _focusDtFechamento.dispose();
    _focusSalvar.dispose();

    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _iniciarNovaCotacao();

    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      _focusDescricao.requestFocus(); // ✅ agora funciona
    });
  }

  void _mostrarErro(String msg) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      SnackBar(
        content: Text(msg),
        backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        duration: const Duration(seconds: 2),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _fechar() => Navigator.pop(context);

  Future<void> salvar() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    if (_numeroCotacao == null) {
      _mostrarErro('Cotação ainda não foi inicializada');
      return;
    }

    try {
      await CotacaoService.atualizarDatas(
        empresa: 1,
        cotId: int.parse(_numeroCotacao!), // agora 100% seguro
        dataAbertura: _dtAberturaCtrl.text,
        dataFechamento: _dtFechamentoCtrl.text,
        descricao: descricaoCtrl.text.trim(),
      );

      Navigator.pop(context, true);
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('Erro ao salvar cotação: $e')),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _iniciarNovaCotacao() async {
    try {
      setState(() => _podeSalvar = false);

      final resp = await CotacaoService.abrirCotacao(
        empresa: 1,
        usuario: 1,
      );

      setState(() {
        _numeroCotacao = resp['cot_id'].toString();
        _dtAberturaCtrl.text = resp['data_abertura'];
      });
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Erro ao abrir nova cotação'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        ),
      );
      _fechar();
    }
  }

  String _hoje() {
    final d = DateTime.now();
    return '${d.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/'
        '${d.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/'
        '${d.year}';
  }

  Future<void> _pickDate(TextEditingController ctrl) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final picked = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: now,
      firstDate: DateTime(now.year - 1),
      lastDate: DateTime(now.year + 5),
    );
    if (picked != null) {
      ctrl.text =
          '${picked.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/${picked.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/${picked.year}';
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Dialog(
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      insetPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Shortcuts(
        shortcuts: {
          const SingleActivator(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape): _BackIntent(),

                                          CrossAxisAlignment.start,

                                          onEditingComplete: () {
                                            _focusDtAbertura
                                                .requestFocus(); // 🔥 PULA PARA DATA ABERTURA
                                          },
                                          decoration: _dec(
                                              'Ex: Cotação semanal – Hortifruti'),

                                            setState(() => _podeSalvar = false);

                                      },
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 14),
                                    const _FieldLabel('DATA FECHAMENTO'),

                                          _pickDate(_dtFechamentoCtrl),

                                        final ok = _validarFechamento();
                                        if (ok) {
                                          _validarFormularioCompleto();

                                          // 🔥 FORÇA FOCO NO BOTÃO SALVAR
                                          WidgetsBinding.instance
                                              .addPostFrameCallback((_) {
                                            _focusSalvar.requestFocus();
                                          });
                                        }
                                        return ok;
                                      },
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),

                            ),
                          )
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

/* ==== COMPONENTES ==== */

class _FieldLabel extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text;
  const _FieldLabel(this.text);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFFE9E4D2),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.black12),
      ),
      child: Text(
        text,
        style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, letterSpacing: .4),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _DateField extends StatelessWidget {
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final FocusNode focusNode;
  final FocusNode? nextFocus;
  final VoidCallback onPick;
  final bool Function() onValidate;

  const _DateField({
    required this.controller,
    required this.focusNode,
    this.nextFocus,
    required this.onPick,
    required this.onValidate,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return TextFormField(
      controller: controller,
      focusNode: focusNode,
      keyboardType: TextInputType.datetime,
      inputFormatters: [
        FilteringTextInputFormatter.allow(RegExp(r'[0-9/]')),
        LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(10),
      ],
      //onChanged: (_) => onChanged(),
      onEditingComplete: () {
        controller.text = normalizarDataDigitada(controller.text);

        final ok = onValidate();

        if (ok && nextFocus != null) {
          nextFocus!.requestFocus();
        }
      },
      decoration: _dec('dd/MM/aaaa').copyWith(
        suffixIcon: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.calendar_month),
          onPressed: onPick,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

String normalizarDataDigitada(String valor) {
  final hoje = DateTime.now();

  String v = valor.replaceAll('/', '').trim();

  if (v.length == 2) {
    // dd
    return '${v}/'
        '${hoje.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/'
        '${hoje.year}';
  }

  if (v.length == 4) {
    // ddMM
    return '${v.substring(0, 2)}/'
        '${v.substring(2, 4)}/'
        '${hoje.year}';
  }

  if (v.length == 6) {
    // ddMMyy
    return '${v.substring(0, 2)}/'
        '${v.substring(2, 4)}/'
        '20${v.substring(4, 6)}';
  }

  return valor; // já digitado completo
}

class _SalvarButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;
  final FocusNode focusNode;

  const _SalvarButton({
    required this.onPressed,
    required this.focusNode,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return FocusableActionDetector(
      focusNode: focusNode,
      shortcuts: const {
        SingleActivator(LogicalKeyboardKey.enter): ActivateIntent(),
        SingleActivator(LogicalKeyboardKey.numpadEnter): ActivateIntent(),
      },
      actions: {
        ActivateIntent: CallbackAction<ActivateIntent>(
          onInvoke: (_) {
            if (onPressed != null) {
              onPressed!();
            }
            return null;
          },
        ),
      },
      child: ElevatedButton(
        onPressed: onPressed,
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: Colors.black,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
          ),
        ),
        child: const Text('SALVAR'),
      ),
    );
  }
}

InputDecoration _dec(String hint) => InputDecoration(
      hintText: hint,
      filled: true,
      fillColor: Colors.white,
      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
    );

class _SaveIntent extends Intent {}

class _BackIntent extends Intent {}












import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import '../../services/api_service.dart';
import 'incluir_fornecedor_dialog.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

const Color _rowEven = Colors.white;
const Color _rowOdd = Color(0xFFFFF6CC); // amarelo claro padrão grid
const Color _rowDivider = Color(0xFFE0E0E0);

class CotacaoFornecedoresPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final int empresa;
  final int cotacaoId;

  const CotacaoFornecedoresPage({
    super.key,
    required this.empresa,
    required this.cotacaoId,
  });

  @override
  State<CotacaoFornecedoresPage> createState() =>
      _CotacaoFornecedoresPageState();
}

class _CotacaoFornecedoresPageState extends State<CotacaoFornecedoresPage> {
  List<Map<String, dynamic>> fornecedores = [];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    carregarFornecedores();
  }

  Future<void> carregarFornecedores() async {
    final resp = await http.get(
      Uri.parse(
        'http://localhost:5003/cotacoes/${widget.cotacaoId}/fornecedores'
        '?empresa=${widget.empresa}',
      ),
    );

    ApiService.validarResposta(resp);

    setState(() {
      fornecedores = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(jsonDecode(resp.body));
    });
  }

  Future<void> _abrirIncluirFornecedor() async {
    await showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (_) => IncluirFornecedorDialog(
        empresa: widget.empresa,
        cotacaoId: widget.cotacaoId,
        onIncluido: carregarFornecedores,
      ),
    );
  }

  // 🗑️ EXCLUIR FORNECEDOR (IGUAL PRODUTOS)
  Future<void> _excluirFornecedor(Map<String, dynamic> fornecedor) async {
    await showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (ctx) {
        bool carregando = false;

        return StatefulBuilder(
          builder: (ctx, setState) {
            return AlertDialog(
              title: const Text('Confirmar exclusão'),
              content: carregando
                  ? const SizedBox(
                      height: 48,
                      child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                    )
                  : const Text(
                      'Deseja realmente excluir este fornecedor da cotação?',
                    ),
              actions: [
                TextButton(
                  onPressed: carregando ? null : () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
                  child: const Text('Cancelar'),
                ),
                ElevatedButton(
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                  ),
                  onPressed: carregando
                      ? null
                      : () async {
                          setState(() => carregando = true);

                          try {
                            await ApiService.post(
                              'http://localhost:5003/cotacoes/fornecedores/excluir',
                              {
                                'empresa': widget.empresa,
                                'cotacao': widget.cotacaoId,
                                'fornecedor': fornecedor['id'],
                              },
                            );

                            await carregarFornecedores();
                            Navigator.pop(ctx);

                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              const SnackBar(
                                content:
                                    Text('Fornecedor excluído com sucesso'),
                              ),
                            );
                          } on ApiException catch (e) {
                            setState(() => carregando = false);

                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              SnackBar(
                                content: Text(e.message),
                                backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                              ),
                            );
                          } catch (e) {
                            setState(() => carregando = false);

                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                              const SnackBar(
                                content: Text(
                                    'Erro inesperado ao excluir fornecedor'),
                                backgroundColor: Colors.red,
                              ),
                            );
                          }
                        },
                  child: const Text('Excluir'),
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final total = fornecedores.length.toString().padLeft(3, '0');

    return FocusableActionDetector(
      autofocus: true,
      shortcuts: const {
        SingleActivator(LogicalKeyboardKey.f2): ActivateIntent(),
      },
      actions: {
        ActivateIntent: CallbackAction<Intent>(
          onInvoke: (_) {
            _abrirIncluirFornecedor();
            return null;
          },
        ),
      },
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF2F2F2),
        body: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
          child: Center(
            child: ConstrainedBox(
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 1200),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  // 🔝 HEADER PADRÃO
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      TextButton.icon(
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
                        label: const Text('Voltar'),
                      ),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                          children: [
                            const Text(
                              'Fornecedores',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                fontSize: 16,
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: 24),
                            Text(
                              'Qtde: $total',
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                fontSize: 15,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      ElevatedButton.icon(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                        label: const Text('Incluir - F2'),
                        onPressed: _abrirIncluirFornecedor,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),

                  // 📋 GRID
                  Expanded(
                    child: SingleChildScrollView(
                      scrollDirection: Axis.vertical,
                      child: DataTable(
                        headingRowColor: MaterialStateProperty.all(
                          Colors.grey.shade200,
                        ),
                        dataRowHeight: 42,
                        headingRowHeight: 40,
                        dividerThickness: 0.6,
                        columns: const [
                          DataColumn(label: Text('Código')),
                          DataColumn(label: Text('Razão Social')),
                          DataColumn(label: Text('Fantasia')),
                          DataColumn(label: Text('CNPJ')),
                          DataColumn(label: Text('Telefone')),
                          DataColumn(label: Text('Ações')),
                        ],
                        rows: List.generate(fornecedores.length, (index) {
                          final f = fornecedores[index];

                          return DataRow(
                            color: MaterialStateProperty.all(
                              index.isEven ? _rowOdd : _rowEven,
                            ),
                            cells: [
                              DataCell(Text(f['id'].toString())),
                              DataCell(Text(f['razao'] ?? '')),
                              DataCell(Text(f['fantasia'] ?? '')),
                              DataCell(Text(f['cnpj'] ?? '')),
                              DataCell(Text(f['telefone'] ?? '')),
                              DataCell(
                                IconButton(
                                  icon: const Icon(
                                    Icons.delete,
                                    color: Colors.red,
                                  ),
                                  onPressed: () => _excluirFornecedor(f),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          );
                        }),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

class CotacaoNovaPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const CotacaoNovaPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<CotacaoNovaPage> createState() => _CotacaoNovaPageState();
}

class _CotacaoNovaPageState extends State<CotacaoNovaPage> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  final _numeroCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _descricaoCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _dtAberturaCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _dtFechamentoCtrl = TextEditingController();

  final _df = DateFormat('dd/MM/yyyy');

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _numeroCtrl.dispose();
    _descricaoCtrl.dispose();
    _dtAberturaCtrl.dispose();
    _dtFechamentoCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _pickDate(TextEditingController ctrl) async {
    DateTime initial = DateTime.now();

    // se já tem data preenchida, tenta usar como inicial
    try {
      if (ctrl.text.trim().isNotEmpty) {
        initial = _df.parse(ctrl.text.trim());
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    final dt = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: initial,
      firstDate: DateTime(2000),
      lastDate: DateTime(2100),
    );

    if (dt != null) {
      setState(() => ctrl.text = _df.format(dt));
    }
  }

  Widget _campo({
    required String label,
    required TextEditingController controller,
    TextInputType? keyboardType,
    int maxLines = 1,
    bool readOnly = false,
    VoidCallback? onTap,
    String? Function(String?)? validator,
  }) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        // CAPTION “na linha arredondada”
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.06),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
            border: Border.all(color: Colors.black12),
          ),
          child: Text(
            label.toUpperCase(),
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Colors.black87,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),

        // CAMPO branco, borda arredondada
        TextFormField(
          controller: controller,
          keyboardType: keyboardType,
          maxLines: maxLines,
          readOnly: readOnly,
          onTap: onTap,
          validator: validator,
          decoration: InputDecoration(
            filled: true,
            fillColor: Colors.white,
            contentPadding:
                const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 14),
            border: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
              borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.black26),
            ),
            enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
              borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.black26),
            ),
            focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
              borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Colors.black87, width: 1.3),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  void _salvar() {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    // por enquanto só valida e mostra ok
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(content: Text("Cotação pronta para salvar ✅")),
    );

    // Depois a gente liga na API / Firebird (insert/gerar número etc.)
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      // Fundo amarelinho degradê
      body: Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        height: double.infinity,
        decoration: const BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            colors: [Color(0xFFFFF9E3), Color(0xFFF7EFC7)],
            begin: Alignment.topCenter,
            end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
          ),
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Column(
            children: [
              // Topo com setinha + título
              Padding(
                padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    IconButton(
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.black87),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    const Text(
                      "Nova Cotação",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 22,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        color: Colors.black87,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),

              // Conteúdo
              Expanded(
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 10, 16, 16),
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.55),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
                      border: Border.all(color: Colors.black12),
                    ),
                    child: Form(
                      key: _formKey,
                      child: Column(
                        children: [
                          _campo(
                            label: "Número da cotação",
                            controller: _numeroCtrl,
                            keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                            validator: (v) {
                              if (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) {
                                return "Informe o número da cotação";
                              }
                              return null;
                            },
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 14),

                          _campo(
                            label: "Descrição",
                            controller: _descricaoCtrl,
                            maxLines: 3,
                            validator: (v) {
                              if (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) {
                                return "Informe a descrição";
                              }
                              return null;
                            },
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 14),

                          _campo(
                            label: "Data Abertura",
                            controller: _dtAberturaCtrl,
                            readOnly: true,
                            onTap: () => _pickDate(_dtAberturaCtrl),
                            validator: (v) {
                              if (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) {
                                return "Informe a data de abertura";
                              }
                              return null;
                            },
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 14),

                          _campo(
                            label: "Data Fechamento",
                            controller: _dtFechamentoCtrl,
                            readOnly: true,
                            onTap: () => _pickDate(_dtFechamentoCtrl),
                            validator: (v) {
                              if (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) {
                                return "Informe a data de fechamento";
                              }
                              return null;
                            },
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 22),

                          // Botões preto/branco (padrão Parmasoft)
                          Align(
                            alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                            child: SizedBox(
                              width:
                                  160, // <-- ajuste o tamanho aqui (140/160/180)
                              child: ElevatedButton(
                                onPressed: _salvar,
                                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                                  backgroundColor: Colors.black,
                                  padding:
                                      const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                                  ),
                                ),
                                child: const Text(
                                  "SALVAR",
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}










# --------------------------------------------------
# DADOS DA COTAÇÃO PELO TOKEN  (já é parte do HTML)
# --------------------------------------------------
@app.get("/cotacoes/token/dados/<token>")
def dados_cotacao_token(token):
    con = None
    try:
        con = get_connection()
        cur = con.cursor()

        # 1️⃣ Buscar e validar token
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT DISTINCT 
                CTK_EMPRESA,
                CTK_COTACAO,
                CTK_FORNECEDOR,
                CTK_EXPIRA_EM,
                CTK_UTILIZADO
            FROM TB_COTACAO_TOKEN
            WHERE CTK_TOKEN = ?
        """, (token,))

        row = cur.fetchone()

        if not row:
            return jsonify({"erro": "Token inválido"}), 404

        empresa, cotacao, fornecedor, expira_em, utilizado = row

        if utilizado == 'S':
            return jsonify({"erro": "Token já utilizado"}), 403

        # 2️⃣ Buscar dados básicos da cotação
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT DISTINCT 
                C.COT_DESCRICAO,
                C.COT_DT_FECHAMENTO
            FROM TB_COTACAO C
            WHERE C.COT_EMPRESA = ?
              AND C.COT_ID = ?
        """, (empresa, cotacao))

        cot = cur.fetchone()
        if not cot:
            return jsonify({"erro": "Cotação não encontrada"}), 404

        descricao, data_fechamento = cot

        # 3️⃣ Buscar itens da cotação
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT DISTINCT 
                L.COTL_PRODUTO,
                P.PRD_DESCRICAO
            FROM TB_COTACAO_LANCAMENTO L
            JOIN TB_PRODUTO P
              ON P.PRD_EMPRESA = L.COTL_EMPRESA
             AND P.PRD_ID = L.COTL_PRODUTO
            WHERE L.COTL_EMPRESA = ?
              AND L.COTL_COTACAO = ?
            ORDER BY P.PRD_DESCRICAO
        """, (empresa, cotacao))

        itens = []
        for prod_id, descricao_prod in cur.fetchall():
            itens.append({
                "produto_id": prod_id,
                "descricao": descricao_prod
            })

        return jsonify({
            "ok": True,
            "empresa": empresa,
            "cotacao": cotacao,
            "descricao": descricao,
            "validade": data_fechamento.strftime("%d/%m/%Y") if data_fechamento else None,
            "fornecedor": fornecedor,
            "itens": itens
        })

    except Exception as e:
        print("Erro dados cotacao token:", e)
        return jsonify({"erro": "Erro ao buscar dados da cotação"}), 500
    finally:
        if con:
            con.close()

from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
import fdb

def gerar_token():
    # token seguro, impossível de adivinhar
    return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)  
  
    
app = Flask(__name__)

# ==================================================
# UTIL – PARSE DE DATA (YYYY-MM-DD ou DD/MM/YYYY)
# ==================================================
def parse_date(value):
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, date):
        return value

    s = str(value).strip()
    if not s:
        return None

    for fmt in ("%Y-%m-%d", "%d/%m/%Y"):
        try:
            return datetime.strptime(s, fmt).date()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return None


# ==================================================
# ABRIR NOVA COTAÇÃO
# ==================================================
@app.route("/cotacoes/abrir", methods=["POST"])
def abrir_cotacao():
    data = request.json or {}

    empresa = data.get("empresa")
    usuario = data.get("usuario")

    if not empresa or not usuario:
        return jsonify({"erro": "Empresa ou usuário não informado"}), 400

    con = None
    try:
        con = get_connection()
        cur = con.cursor()

        cur.execute("""
            INSERT INTO TB_COTACAO (
                COT_EMPRESA,
                COT_STATUS,
                COT_DT_ABERTURA,
                COT_HORA_ABERTURA,
                COT_DT_LANCAMENTO,
                COT_USUARIO
            ) VALUES (?, ?, CURRENT_DATE, CURRENT_TIME, CURRENT_DATE, ?)
        """, (
            empresa,
            'A',
            usuario
        ))

        cur.execute("""
            SELECT COT_ID
            FROM TB_COTACAO
            WHERE COT_EMPRESA = ?
            ORDER BY COT_ID DESC
            ROWS 1
        """, (empresa,))

        row = cur.fetchone()
        cot_id = row[0] if row else None

        con.commit()

        return jsonify({
            "status": "ok",
            "cot_id": cot_id,
            "data_abertura": date.today().strftime("%d/%m/%Y")
        })

    except Exception as e:
        if con:
            con.rollback()
        print("Erro abrir cotação:", e)
        return jsonify({"erro": "Erro ao abrir cotação"}), 500
    finally:
        if con:
            con.close()
