      final resp = await CotacaoService.abrirCotacao(
        empresa: 1, // depois vem do contexto
        usuario: 1, // depois vem do login
      );

      setState(() {
        _numeroCotacao = resp['cot_id'].toString();
        _dtAberturaCtrl.text = resp['data_abertura'];
      });
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('Erro ao abrir nova cotação'),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
        ),
      );
      _fechar();
    }
  }

  String _hoje() {
    final d = DateTime.now();
    return '${d.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/'
        '${d.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/'
        '${d.year}';
  }

  Future<void> _pickDate(TextEditingController ctrl) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final picked = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: now,
      firstDate: DateTime(now.year - 1),
      lastDate: DateTime(now.year + 5),
    );
    if (picked != null) {
      ctrl.text =
          '${picked.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/${picked.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/${picked.year}';
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Dialog(
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      insetPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Shortcuts(
        shortcuts: {
          const SingleActivator(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape): _BackIntent(),
          const SingleActivator(LogicalKeyboardKey.keyS, control: true):
              _SaveIntent(),
        },
        child: Actions(
          actions: {
            _BackIntent: CallbackAction(onInvoke: (_) => _fechar()),
            _SaveIntent: CallbackAction(onInvoke: (_) => _salvar()),
          },
          child: Material(
            color: Colors.transparent,
            child: Container(
              width: 1100,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                gradient: const LinearGradient(
                  begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                  end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                  colors: [
                    Color(0xFFFFF3C4),
                    Color(0xFFFFF9E6),
                  ],
                ),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: Colors.black.withOpacity(0.25),
                    blurRadius: 20,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 10),
                  )
                ],
              ),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  // CABEÇALHO
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      IconButton(
                        onPressed: _fechar,
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 6),
                      const Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          'Voltar',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 18,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 12),

                  // PAINEL BRANCO
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.8),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                      border: Border.all(color: Colors.black12),
                    ),
                    child: Form(
                      key: _formKey,
                      child: Column(
                        children: [
                          // TOPO
                          Row(
                            children: [
                              const Expanded(
                                child: Text(
                                  'Dados da Cotação',
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 18,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              Container(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                    horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: Colors.black,
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                                ),
                                child: Text(
                                  'Nº: ${_numeroCotacao ?? "..."}',
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                    fontSize: 16,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: 8),
                              Tooltip(
                                message: 'API: ${AppConfig.baseUrl}',
                                child: const Icon(Icons.info_outline),
                              )
                            ],
                          ),

                          const SizedBox(height: 16),

                          Row(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                child: Column(
                                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    const _FieldLabel('DATA ABERTURA'),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                                    _DateField(
                                      controller: _dtAberturaCtrl,
                                      focusNode: _focusDtAbertura,
                                      nextFocus: _focusDtFechamento,
                                      onPick: () => _pickDate(_dtAberturaCtrl),
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 14),
                                    const _FieldLabel('DATA FECHAMENTO'),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                                    _DateField(
                                      controller: _dtFechamentoCtrl,
                                      focusNode: _focusDtFechamento,
                                      nextFocus: _focusSalvar,
                                      onPick: () =>
                                          _pickDate(_dtFechamentoCtrl),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),

                          const SizedBox(height: 18),

                          Align(
                            alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                            child: SizedBox(
                              width: 160,
                              child: _SalvarButton(
                                onPressed: _salvar,
                                focusNode: _focusSalvar,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),

                          const SizedBox(height: 10),
                          const Align(
                            alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                            child: Text(
                              'Atalhos: Ctrl+S Salvar • Esc Voltar',
                              style: TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: 12, color: Colors.black54),
                            ),
                          )
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/* ==== COMPONENTES ==== */

class _FieldLabel extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text;
  const _FieldLabel(this.text);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFFE9E4D2),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.black12),
      ),
      child: Text(
        text,
        style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, letterSpacing: .4),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _DateField extends StatelessWidget {
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final FocusNode focusNode;
  final FocusNode? nextFocus;
  final VoidCallback onPick;

  const _DateField({
    required this.controller,
    required this.focusNode,
    this.nextFocus,
    required this.onPick,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return TextFormField(
      controller: controller,
      focusNode: focusNode,
      keyboardType: TextInputType.datetime,
      inputFormatters: [
        FilteringTextInputFormatter.allow(RegExp(r'[0-9/]')),
        LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(10),
      ],
      onEditingComplete: () {
        controller.text = normalizarDataDigitada(controller.text);
        if (nextFocus != null) {
          nextFocus!.requestFocus();
        }
      },
      onTapOutside: (_) {
        controller.text = normalizarDataDigitada(controller.text);
      },
      decoration: _dec('dd/MM/aaaa').copyWith(
        suffixIcon: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.calendar_month),
          onPressed: onPick,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

String normalizarDataDigitada(String valor) {
  final hoje = DateTime.now();

  String v = valor.replaceAll('/', '').trim();

  if (v.length == 2) {
    // dd
    return '${v}/'
        '${hoje.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/'
        '${hoje.year}';
  }

  if (v.length == 4) {
    // ddMM
    return '${v.substring(0, 2)}/'
        '${v.substring(2, 4)}/'
        '${hoje.year}';
  }

  if (v.length == 6) {
    // ddMMyy
    return '${v.substring(0, 2)}/'
        '${v.substring(2, 4)}/'
        '20${v.substring(4, 6)}';
  }

  return valor; // já digitado completo
}

class _SalvarButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final VoidCallback onPressed;
  final FocusNode focusNode;

  const _SalvarButton({
    required this.onPressed,
    required this.focusNode,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ElevatedButton(
      focusNode: focusNode,
      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
        backgroundColor: Colors.black,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
        ),
      ),
      onPressed: onPressed,
      child: const Text('SALVAR'),
    );
  }
}

InputDecoration _dec(String hint) => InputDecoration(
      hintText: hint,
      filled: true,
      fillColor: Colors.white,
      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
    );

class _SaveIntent extends Intent {}

class _BackIntent extends Intent {}








cotação servisse      11/01      0822

import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:cotacao_online/app/app_config.dart';

class CotacaoService {
  static Future<Map<String, dynamic>> abrirCotacao({
    required int empresa,
    required int usuario,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('${AppConfig.baseUrl}/cotacoes/abrir');

    final response = await http.post(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({
        'empresa': empresa,
        'usuario': usuario,
      }),
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw Exception('Erro ao abrir cotação');
    }

    return jsonDecode(response.body);
  }





  static Future<void> atualizarDataFechamento({
    required int empresa,
    required int cotId,
    required String dataFechamento,
  }) async {
    final url = Uri.parse('${AppConfig.baseUrl}/cotacoes/fechamento');

    final response = await http.post(
      url,
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: jsonEncode({
        'empresa': empresa,
        'cot_id': cotId,
        'data_fechamento': dataFechamento,
      }),
    );

    if (response.statusCode != 200) {
      throw Exception('Erro ao atualizar data de fechamento');
    }
  }
}

class CotacaoNovaDesktopModal extends StatefulWidget {
  const CotacaoNovaDesktopModal({super.key});

  @override
  State<CotacaoNovaDesktopModal> createState() =>
      _CotacaoNovaDesktopModalState();
}

class _CotacaoNovaDesktopModalState extends State<CotacaoNovaDesktopModal> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  final _descricaoCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _dtAberturaCtrl = TextEditingController();
  final _dtFechamentoCtrl = TextEditingController();

  String? _numeroCotacao;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _descricaoCtrl.dispose();
    _dtAberturaCtrl.dispose();
    _dtFechamentoCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _fechar() => Navigator.pop(context);

  Future<void> _salvar() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(content: Text('Salvar — em construção')),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _pickDate(TextEditingController ctrl) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final picked = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: now,
      firstDate: DateTime(now.year - 1),
      lastDate: DateTime(now.year + 5),
    );
    if (picked != null) {
      ctrl.text =
          '${picked.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/${picked.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}/${picked.year}';
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Dialog(
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      insetPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
      child: Shortcuts(
        shortcuts: {
          const SingleActivator(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape): _BackIntent(),
          const SingleActivator(LogicalKeyboardKey.keyS, control: true):
              _SaveIntent(),
        },
        child: Actions(
          actions: {

                  )
                ],
              ),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  // CABEÇALHO
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      IconButton(
                        onPressed: _fechar,
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),

                      const SizedBox(width: 6),

                        child: Text(
                          'Voltar',

                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 12),

                  // PAINEL BRANCO
                  Container(

                                child: Text(

class _FieldLabel extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text;
  const _FieldLabel(this.text);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: const Color(0xFFE9E4D2),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.black12),
      ),
      child: Text(text,
          style:
              const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, letterSpacing: .4)),
    );
  }
}

class _DateField extends StatelessWidget {
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final VoidCallback onPick;

  const _DateField({required this.controller, required this.onPick});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return TextFormField(
      controller: controller,
      readOnly: true,
      onTap: onPick,
      decoration: _dec('dd/MM/aaaa').copyWith(
        suffixIcon: IconButton(
          icon: const Icon(Icons.calendar_month),
          onPressed: onPick,
        ),
      ),
      validator: (v) => v == null || v.isEmpty ? 'Informe a data' : null,
    );
  }
}

class _SalvarButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final VoidCallback onPressed;
  const _SalvarButton({required this.onPressed});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ElevatedButton(
      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
        backgroundColor: Colors.black,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999)),
      ),
      onPressed: onPressed,
      child: const Text('SALVAR'),
    );
  }
}

InputDecoration _dec(String hint) => InputDecoration(
      hintText: hint,
      filled: true,
      fillColor: Colors.white,
      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
    );
